中文摘要翻译: 心电图（ECG）和脑电图（EEG）等医疗时间序列表现出复杂的时序动态性和结构化的跨通道依赖性，给自动化分析带来了根本性挑战。传统的卷积和循环模型难以捕获长程依赖，而基于Transformer的方法则面临二次复杂度问题，并且常常引入与生理信号内在结构不匹配的冗余交互。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了MedMamba——一种面向医疗时间序列分类的先验驱动多尺度双向状态空间架构。我们的设计遵循生理信号的三种关键归纳偏置：空间集中化、多时间尺度时序组成和非因果上下文依赖。这些先验通过轻量级通道混合模块实现跨通道重参数化、多尺度卷积分词实现时间分解、以及双向Mamba模块以线性复杂度实现高效全局上下文建模。在涵盖EEG、ECG和人体活动信号的六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MedMamba在多种模态上持续优于现有最优方法。值得注意的是，它在PTB上达到85.97%的准确率，并在具有挑战性的ADFTD数据集上取得了新的最优性能（准确率54.72%，F1分数52.01%）。在SleepEDF等长序列基准上的优异结果进一步验证了其在长程依赖建模方面的能力。此外，MedMamba实现了4.6倍的推理加速，突显了其在实时临床部署中的实用性。这些结果表明，先验引导的状态空间建模为医疗时间序列分析提供了一种高效且可扩展的Transformer替代方案。

</details>


### [89] [Memory-Efficient EDA Denoising via Knowledge Distillation for Wearable IoT Under Severe Motion Artifacts and Underwater Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05246)
*Yongbin Lee,Andrew Peitzsch,Youngsun Kong,Jarod Zizza,Dong-hee Kang,Farnoush Baghestani,Ki H. Chon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种结合知识蒸馏与CNN-Transformer混合模型的EDA信号去噪框架，在显著压缩模型体积和计算成本的同时，有效去除水下等恶劣环境中的运动伪影和噪声，提升了皮肤电活动信号质量及临床相关的早期预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: EDA信号在可穿戴医疗物联网系统中广泛用于自主神经评估，但极易受到运动伪影和环境噪声干扰，特别是在水下等恶劣环境中，现有方法难以可靠部署。因此需要一种能在多种测量位置和恶劣环境下泛化的鲁棒、可部署的EDA去噪框架。

Method: 提出一个混合CNN-Transformer教师模型，并通过知识蒸馏策略将知识迁移到一个轻量级深度可分离CNN学生模型；同时引入一种逼真的数据增广方案，模拟多种运动伪影和环境失真，以提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 学生模型大小从7.87 MB压缩至0.51 MB，计算成本从105.1M降至11.61M FLOPs，在公开数据集上MAE为0.144、SNR提升12.08 dB；在真实水下环境下MAE从2.809降至0.215；在CNS-OT数据集上AUROC达0.806，早期预测灵敏度从0.550提升至0.767，中位预测时间提前6.9分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅提升了EDA信号质量，还增强了临床相关的预测性能，同时适用于资源受限的可穿戴物联网系统在恶劣环境中的部署。

Original Abstract: Electrodermal activity (EDA) is widely used in wearable Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) systems for continuous health monitoring, including autonomic assessment. However, EDA signals are highly vulnerable to motion artifacts and environmental noise, limiting reliable deployment in harsh operating conditions such as underwater. This study proposes a robust, deployable EDA denoising framework that generalizes across multiple measurement locations and harsh environments. The framework integrates a hybrid CNN-Transformer teacher model with a lightweight depth-wise separable CNN student model via a knowledge distillation (KD) strategy. To further improve robustness, a realistic data augmentation scheme is introduced to simulate diverse motion artifacts and environmental distortions. The KD-based student model significantly reduces model size (7.87 MB to 0.51 MB) and computational cost (105.1M to 11.61M FLOPs) while maintaining denoising performance (MAE: 0.144, SNR improvement: 12.08 dB) using the public dataset validation. In real-world underwater conditions (UMAC dataset) testing, the proposed method substantially improves skin conductance response reconstruction, reducing mean absolute error from 2.809 to 0.215. Furthermore, on independent testing using the CNS-OT dataset, the denoised signals enhanced downstream CNS-OT prediction performance, achieving the highest AUROC (0.806) compared to prior denoising methods. The proposed method also improved the early prediction rate (sensitivity) from 0.550 to 0.767, enabling CNS-OT prediction up to a median of 6.9 minutes before symptom onset. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework not only improves EDA signal quality but also enhances clinically relevant prediction performance while remaining suitable for deployment in resource-constrained wearable Internet of Things systems operating in harsh environments.

中文摘要翻译: 皮肤电活动（EDA）广泛用于可穿戴医疗物联网（IoMT）系统以实现连续健康监测，包括自主神经评估。然而，EDA信号极易受到运动伪影和环境噪声的影响，限制了其在水下等恶劣操作条件下的可靠部署。本研究提出了一种鲁棒且可部署的EDA去噪框架，能够在多种测量位置和恶劣环境下泛化。该框架通过知识蒸馏（KD）策略，将混合CNN-Transformer教师模型的知识迁移到轻量级深度可分离CNN学生模型中。为进一步提升鲁棒性，引入了一种逼真的数据增广方案，以模拟多样的运动伪影和环境失真。基于KD的学生模型在保持去噪性能（MAE：0.144，SNR提升：12.08 dB）的同时，显著减少了模型大小（7.87 MB降至0.51 MB）和计算成本（105.1M降至11.61M FLOPs），该结果通过公开数据集验证。在真实水下环境（UMAC数据集）测试中，所提方法显著改善了皮肤电导反应重建，将平均绝对误差从2.809降低至0.215。此外，在基于CNS-OT数据集的独立测试中，去噪后的信号提升了下游CNS-OT预测性能，达到了与先前去噪方法相比最高的AUROC（0.806）。所提方法还将早期预测率（灵敏度）从0.550提升至0.767，使得CNS-OT预测的中位时间提前至症状发作前6.9分钟。这些结果表明，所提框架不仅改善了EDA信号质量，还增强了临床相关的预测性能，同时适用于部署在恶劣环境下资源受限的可穿戴物联网系统中。

</details>


### [90] [Covariance Square Root Second-Order Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05560)
*Keith A. LeGrand,Braden Hastings,Jackson Kulik*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了二阶协方差映射的平方根计算方法，用于解决递归状态估计中因有限精度算术导致的数值误差问题，相较于全协方差矩阵计算，该方法不仅精度更高，且通常需要更少的浮点运算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在递归状态估计中，有限精度算术产生的舍入误差会破坏理论保证，导致协方差矩阵不对称或非正定。现有平方根分解技术仅适用于一阶协方差映射（如扩展卡尔曼滤波），无法直接扩展到在非线性场景中表现优异的高阶矩映射，因此需要针对二阶协方差映射开发平方根计算方法。

Method: 提出了二阶协方差映射的平方根计算方法，即对协方差矩阵进行平方根分解，在更新过程中直接操作平方根因子而非完整协方差矩阵，从而保持数值稳定性和正定性。

Result: 在两个不同的数值实验中，该方法相比全协方差矩阵计算具有更高的精度，且通常需要更少的浮点运算次数。

Conclusion: 平方根计算方法可以有效地扩展到二阶协方差映射，在提高数值精度的同时降低计算成本，为高阶矩映射在高度非线性环境中的应用提供了更可靠的数值实现方案。

Original Abstract: In recursive state estimation, numerical error can play a major role in an algorithm's overall performance and reliability. Roundoff errors due to finite precision arithmetic can violate theoretical guarantees, leading to asymmetric and non-positive-semidefinite covariance matrices. In algorithms employing first-order covariance mappings, such as the extended Kalman filter, these issues have been mitigated by employing square root factorizations of the covariance matrix. However, existing techniques do not directly extend to higher-order moment mappings, which show great value in highly nonlinear settings. This paper presents the first known square root computation of the second-order covariance mapping. The square root computation is not only more accurate, as is shown in two distinct numerical experiments, but generally requires fewer floating point operations compared to the full covariance matrix computation.

中文摘要翻译: 在递归状态估计中，数值误差对算法的整体性能和可靠性起着重要作用。有限精度算术导致的舍入误差会破坏理论保证，产生不对称且非正定的协方差矩阵。在使用一阶协方差映射的算法中（如扩展卡尔曼滤波），这些问题已通过采用协方差矩阵的平方根分解得到缓解。然而，现有技术无法直接扩展到在高度非线性场景中展现出重要价值的高阶矩映射。本文首次提出了二阶协方差映射的平方根计算方法。两个不同的数值实验表明，该平方根计算方法不仅精度更高，而且与全协方差矩阵计算相比，通常需要更少的浮点运算次数。

</details>


### [91] [Optimizing In Vivo Oral Lesion Classification from Electrical Impedance Spectroscopy Using Data-driven Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05729)
*Sophie A. Lloyd,Jacob P. Thönes,Safina S. Suratwala,Noor Zaghlula,Liang Lu,Joseph Paydarfar,Ethan K. Murphy,Sascha Spors,Ryan J. Halter*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种机器学习管道，通过降维和特征优化，利用电阻抗谱（EIS）数据实现口腔癌的实时、非侵入性分类，在二分类任务中达到80%准确率和0.90 AUC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 口腔癌是全球重大健康负担，早期检测至关重要。电阻抗谱（EIS）作为一种非侵入性实时组织表征技术具有潜力，但目前尚缺乏能够联合利用多种阻抗特征进行口腔病变分类的框架。

Method: 从104名患者中采集EIS数据，使用PCA对信号频率进行独立排序和降维（最高降幅达99%），测试不同电流注入/电压测量（IIVV）模式几何结构，采用留一患者组交叉验证评估性能，对比逻辑回归等模型。

Result: 逻辑回归在健康 vs 癌症二分类中达到80%准确率和0.90 AUC；多分类场景AUC均高于0.82；降维后性能优于全量基线模型；最优模型均使用显著降维后的IIVV特征集。

Conclusion: 该管道提供了一种鲁棒、计算高效且临床实用的口腔癌早期诊断框架，其方法可推广至其他EIS设备与应用。

Original Abstract: Oral cancer is a significant global health burden, and early detection remains a critical clinical need. Electrical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) offers a promising non-invasive approach for real-time tissue characterization, but classification frameworks that jointly leverage multiple impedance features for in vivo oral lesion discrimination remain underdeveloped. This paper presents a machine-learning (ML) pipeline to optimize classification of in vivo oral pathology from EIS data collected using a handheld, bedside device. Impedance measurements were acquired from 104 patients undergoing oral cancer resection or biopsy. Three classification tasks were evaluated: (1) healthy vs. cancer, (2) multi-class lesion-type discrimination (cancer, high-grade dysplasia, non-malignant), and (3) multi-class discrimination between the three lesion pathologies and healthy tissue. For each task, signal frequencies were independently ranked and reduced using PCA, and different current injection/voltage measurement (IIVV) pattern geometries were tested. Classification performance was assessed through leave-one-patient-group-out cross-validation to ensure robustness on unseen patients. Input data dimensionality was reduced by up to 99% across all tasks while improving diagnostic accuracy over baseline models trained on the full dataset. A logistic regression model achieved the highest binary classification accuracy of 80% with an AUC of 0.90, while multi-class scenarios maintained AUCs above 0.82. All top-performing models utilized the significantly reduced IIVV set as input. The proposed pipeline advances EIS-based cancer detection by providing a robust, computationally efficient, and clinically practical framework for early diagnosis of oral cancer lesions, with a methodology readily generalizable to other EIS devices and applications.

中文摘要翻译: 口腔癌是全球重大的健康负担，早期检测仍然是关键的临床需求。电阻抗谱（EIS）提供了一种有前景的非侵入性方法，可用于实时组织表征，但目前仍缺乏能够联合利用多种阻抗特征进行口腔病变体内分类的框架。本文提出了一种机器学习（ML）管道，用于优化基于EIS数据的口腔病理体内分类，数据来自一台手持式床旁设备。阻抗测量采集自104名接受口腔癌切除或活检的患者。评估了三个分类任务：（1）健康 vs. 癌症；（2）多类别病变类型区分（癌症、高级别不典型增生、非恶性）；（3）三种病变病理与健康组织之间的多类别区分。对于每个任务，使用PCA对信号频率进行独立排序和降维，并测试了不同的电流注入/电压测量（IIVV）模式几何结构。通过留一患者组交叉验证评估分类性能，以确保对未见患者的鲁棒性。所有任务的输入数据维度降低了高达99%，同时相比基于完整数据训练的基线模型提高了诊断准确性。逻辑回归模型在二分类中达到了最高的80%准确率和0.90的AUC，多分类场景的AUC保持在0.82以上。所有表现最优的模型均使用显著降维后的IIVV特征集作为输入。所提出的管道通过提供一种鲁棒、计算高效且临床实用的口腔癌早期诊断框架，推动了基于EIS的癌症检测，其方法学可推广至其他EIS设备与应用。

</details>


### [92] [Bayesian Learning-Aided Near-Field Channel Estimation for mmWave Hybrid MIMO systems employing Uniform Circular Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06039)
*Abhisha Garg,Priya Gupta,Suraj Srivastava,Aditya Jagannatham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Ring-Bayes信道学习框架，将贝叶斯学习与近场信道估计统一，针对毫米波混合MIMO系统中均匀圆形阵列（UCA）的近场信道，设计同心环码本实现角度-距离联合域的信道特征捕获，显著提升了信道恢复精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着天线数量增加，用户落入近场区域，传统平面波假设不再成立；同时，基站端广泛使用的均匀线性阵列（ULA）不适合大规模部署，而均匀圆形阵列（UCA）具有更优的波束赋形增益和空间指向性。现有方法未能有效利用UCA近场信道特性，亟需新的信道学习框架。

Method: 提出Ring-Bayes框架，将贝叶斯学习与近场信道估计统一；设计近场同心环码本，在角度和距离联合域中捕获信道特征；利用该码本结构实现UCA近场信道的高精度恢复。

Result: 大量仿真表明，所提方法在信道恢复精度上显著优于现有方法，证明了Ring-Bayes作为下一代毫米波通信可扩展解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: Ring-Bayes是一个强大且可扩展的解决方案，适用于下一代毫米波通信系统中的近场信道估计。

Original Abstract: This work conceives a Ring-Bayes channel learning framework that unifies Bayesian learning with near-field channel estimation in millimeter-wave (mmWave) hybrid MIMO systems. As the number of antennas scales up, users increasingly fall within the near-field region, rendering the conventional planar-wave assumption invalid. Moreover, the widely studied uniform linear arrays (ULAs) at the base station are impractical for large-scale deployment, whereas uniform circular arrays (UCAs) achieve superior beamforming gain and spatial directivity with the same antenna aperture. To exploit these advantages, we design a near-field concentric-ring codebook that captures channel features jointly in angular and distance domains. Leveraging this structure, the proposed Ring-Bayes framework enables highly accurate recovery of UCA near-field channels. Extensive simulations confirm that our approach delivers substantial improvements over existing methods, establishing Ring-Bayes as a powerful and scalable solution for next-generation mmWave communications.

中文摘要翻译: 本工作提出了Ring-Bayes信道学习框架，将贝叶斯学习与毫米波混合MIMO系统中的近场信道估计相统一。随着天线数量的增加，用户越来越多地落入近场区域，传统的平面波假设不再成立。此外，基站端广泛研究的均匀线性阵列（ULA）在大规模部署中不切实际，而均匀圆形阵列（UCA）在相同天线孔径下可实现更优的波束赋形增益和空间指向性。为利用这些优势，我们设计了一种近场同心环码本，在角度和距离联合域中捕获信道特征。借助这种结构，所提出的Ring-Bayes框架能够实现UCA近场信道的高精度恢复。大量仿真证实，我们的方法在性能上显著优于现有方法，确立了Ring-Bayes作为下一代毫米波通信系统中一种强大且可扩展的解决方案。

</details>


### [93] [A Family of Hybrid Beyond-Diagonal RIS Architectures: Design and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06107)
*Konstantinos Ntougias,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类混合有源/无源超对角可重构智能表面（BD-RIS）架构，将有源放大与BD-RIS的组连接设计相结合，以更少的反射型放大器实现更高的接收信噪比。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统对角RIS受限于路径损耗和有限的散射矩阵集合，而有源RIS虽能缓解路径损耗但需要大量反射型放大器；BD-RIS通过元件间耦合扩展了散射矩阵空间，但尚未与有源方案结合。本文旨在融合BD-RIS与混合有源/无源设计，在提升信噪比的同时减少放大器使用。

Method: 将表面划分为两个反射子表面（RS），每个子表面采用无源或有源的组连接BD-RIS设计。提出一种闭式SNR最大化求解方法：对每个BD-RIS组，结合Takagi分解特定复对称矩阵与满足反射功率预算的每组合理放大因子，实现最优波束赋形。

Result: 在直接路径受阻的单输入单输出（SISO）链路中，所提混合BD-RIS架构相比传统对角混合RIS架构，在使用显著更少反射型放大器的情况下，能达到相同或更高的接收信噪比。

Conclusion: 混合BD-RIS架构能有效结合有源放大与无源BD-RIS的优势，以更少的硬件开销实现更优的通信性能，为未来RIS系统设计提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RISs) extend conventional diagonal RISs by allowing inter-element coupling, thereby enlarging the set of attainable scattering matrices and improving the achievable signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). On the other hand, hybrid active/passive RISs use reflect-type power amplifiers in a fraction of the elements to alleviate the multiplicative path loss. In this paper, we bring these two ideas together and introduce a \emph{family of hybrid BD-RIS architectures}, in which the surface is partitioned into two reflecting subsurfaces (RSs), each adopting either a passive or an active group-connected BD-RIS design. We derive a closed-form SNR-maximizing solution that combines, for every BD-RIS group, Takagi's factorization of a certain complex symmetric matrix with an optimal per-group amplification factor that satisfies the reflect-power budget. Three architectures within the proposed family (active/passive, fully-connected-active/sub-connected-active, and sub-connected-active/sub-connected-active hybrid BD-RIS) are studied. Numerical results in a single-input single-output (SISO) link with blocked direct path show that the proposed hybrid BD-RIS architectures attain the same or higher receive SNR than their diagonal counterparts while using significantly fewer reflect-type amplifiers.

中文摘要翻译: 超对角可重构智能表面（BD-RIS）通过允许元件间耦合来扩展传统对角RIS，从而增大了可达散射矩阵的集合，提升了可实现信噪比（SNR）。另一方面，混合有源/无源RIS在部分元件中使用反射型功率放大器以缓解乘性路径损耗。本文融合了这两类思想，引入了一类混合BD-RIS架构，其中表面被划分为两个反射子表面（RS），每个子表面采用无源或有源的组连接BD-RIS设计。我们推导出一种闭式SNR最大化求解方法，该方法针对每个BD-RIS组，将特定复对称矩阵的Takagi分解与满足反射功率预算的每组合理放大因子相结合。研究了所提架构族中的三种具体架构（有源/无源混合、全连接有源/子连接无源混合、以及子连接有源/子连接无源混合BD-RIS）。在直射路径受阻的单输入单输出（SISO）链路中的数值结果表明，所提出的混合BD-RIS架构在使用显著更少反射型放大器的情况下，能达到与传统对角架构相同或更高的接收SNR。

</details>


### [94] [Orthogonal Least Squares with Integrated Information Theoretic Criteria for Joint Number of Targets and DoA Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06198)
*Martin Willame,Gilles Monnoyer,François Horlin,Jérôme Louveaux*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出三种将信息论准则（ITC）的模型阶数选择与正交最小二乘（OLS）估计相融合的算法（基于排名的分离选择、基于选择的联合选择、混合排名与选择），用于联合估计目标数量和波达方向（DoA），其中混合ITC-OLS算法在性能上优于其他变体及基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 直接应用ITC进行目标数量估计需要为每个候选模型阶数计算最大似然DoA估计，这涉及对所有角度组合进行多维搜索，计算成本过高。为了降低复杂度，需要将ITC模型阶数选择融入高效的贪心方法（如OLS）中。

Method: 提出了三种ITC-OLS算法：（1）基于排名的分离选择（disjoint rank-based）；（2）基于选择的联合选择（joint selection-based）；（3）混合排名与选择（hybrid rank-and-selection-based）。每种算法分别在AIC和BIC框架下推导实现，将ITC的模型阶数选择嵌入到OLS的贪婪估计过程中。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提出的混合ITC-OLS算法在性能上持续优于其他两种变体以及文献中的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将ITC模型阶数选择与OLS贪婪估计过程融合，可以实现高效且准确的目标数量和DoA联合估计，其中混合策略效果最佳。

Original Abstract: We address the joint estimation of the number of targets and their direction-of-arrivals (DoAs) using antenna arrays. Target-number estimation can be formulated as a model-order selection problem and solved with the information theoretic criteria (ITC). The ITC minimize an objective function that balances a likelihood term and a complexity penalty. However, direct application of the ITC requires maximum-likelihood DoA estimates for each candidate model order, which is computationally prohibitive because it entails a multidimensional search over all angle combinations. To reduce complexity, many radar processing exploit greedy methods such as orthogonal least squares (OLS). In this paper, we explore three distinct methods to integrate the ITC model-order selection into the OLS estimation procedure for joint target-number and DoA estimation. Specifically, we propose the disjoint rank-based, the joint selection-based, and the hybrid rank-and-selection-based ITC-OLS algorithms. Each algorithm is derived under both the Akaike information criterion (AIC) and the Bayesian information criterion (BIC) frameworks. Numerical simulations show that the proposed hybrid ITC-OLS algorithm consistently outperforms both the other proposed variants and a baseline method from the literature.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究利用天线阵列联合估计目标数量及其波达方向（DoA）。目标数量估计可被建模为模型阶数选择问题，并通过信息论准则（ITC）求解。ITC最小化一个平衡似然项与复杂度惩罚项的目标函数。然而，直接应用ITC需要为每个候选模型阶数计算最大似然DoA估计，这涉及对所有角度组合进行多维搜索，计算成本过高。为了降低复杂度，许多雷达处理利用贪心方法，如正交最小二乘（OLS）。本文探索了三种不同的方法，将ITC模型阶数选择融入OLS估计流程中，以实现目标数量和DoA的联合估计。具体而言，我们提出了基于排名的分离选择、基于选择的联合选择以及混合排名与选择的ITC-OLS算法。每种算法均在Akaike信息准则（AIC）和贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）框架下推导得到。数值仿真表明，所提出的混合ITC-OLS算法在性能上持续优于其他两种变体以及文献中的基线方法。

</details>


### [95] [Cooperative Multi-Static Target Localization for ISAC in Cluttered Industrial IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06256)
*Mostafa Nozari,Israel Leyva-Mayorga,Gilberto Berardinelli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向密集工业物联网环境的协作式多静态目标定位新型通感一体化框架，通过轻量级时空调制学习抑制杂波，并结合采样视场感知初始化与经验位置误差界方案自适应选择最优传感节点，实现高精度快速定位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在密集工业物联网环境中，环境杂波严重影响多静态目标定位的精度与效率，现有方法难以在有限传感资源下兼顾快速收敛与高精度定位。

Method: 首先提出轻量级时空调制学习方法抑制杂波；然后提出迭代定位算法，包含采样视场感知初始化方案和经验位置误差界方案，自适应选择最有效的传感节点子集；最后采用可靠性加权最小二乘估计器融合所选接收节点的距离和到达角测量值进行目标定位。

Result: 所提方法在六次感知迭代内快速收敛，将定位均方根误差降低近两个数量级至约45厘米，在相同感知资源预算下显著优于所有基准方法。

Conclusion: 提出的集成通感一体化框架在密集杂波IIoT环境中实现了快速、高精度的协作式多静态目标定位，验证了轻量级学习与自适应节点选择策略的有效性。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) framework for collaborative multi-static target localization in dense Industrial Internet-of-Things (IIoT) environments in the presence of environmental clutter. We first develop a lightweight temporal clutter-suppression learning method to mitigate persistent reflections. Building on this, we propose an iterative localization algorithm that integrates two key components introduced in this work: a sampling-based field-of-view-aware initialization (SFI) scheme and an empirical position error bound (PEB) scheme, which together adaptively identify the most informative subset of sensing nodes. A reliability-aware weighted least-squares estimator is then employed to fuse range and angle-of-arrival measurements from the selected sensing receivers for target localization. Numerical results demonstrate rapid convergence of the proposed method, reducing the localization RMSE by nearly two orders of magnitude within six sensing iterations to about 45 cm, while significantly outperforming all considered benchmarks under the same sensing-resource budget.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新颖的通感一体化框架，用于在存在环境杂波的密集工业物联网环境中进行协作式多静态目标定位。我们首先开发了一种轻量级的时空调制抑制学习方法以减轻持续反射的影响。在此基础上，我们提出了一种迭代定位算法，该算法集成了本文引入的两个关键组件：基于采样的视场感知初始化方案和经验位置误差界方案，二者共同自适应地识别信息量最大的传感节点子集。然后采用可靠性加权最小二乘估计器，融合来自所选传感接收节点的距离和到达角测量值进行目标定位。数值结果表明，所提方法快速收敛，在六次感知迭代内将定位均方根误差降低近两个数量级至约45厘米，在相同感知资源预算下显著优于所有考虑的基准方法。

</details>


### [96] [The frame-level leakage trap: rethinking evaluation protocols for intrinsic image decomposition, with source-separable uncertainty as a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06359)
*Jihwan Woo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文指出MPI Sintel数据集上内在图像分解的评估协议不一致（帧级划分导致数据泄露），量化了这种泄露效应，并倡导采用场景级划分作为标准。同时提出了一种物理信息引导的分解模型，带源可分离的三路异方差不确定性头，验证了通道专业化及其下游实用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有内在图像分解评估协议在MPI Sintel数据集上不一致，帧级划分导致相同场景的相似帧出现在训练集和测试集中，造成数据泄露，影响评估的公正性和可靠性。作者首次量化了这一泄露效应，并倡导更严格的场景级划分标准。

Method: 提出一个物理信息引导的分解模型 I = R∘S + N，包含一个源可分离的三路异方差不确定性头，分别对应朗伯体、纹理和非朗伯体三个通道的不确定性估计。通过对比帧级与场景级划分的差异，以及三点梯度（随机/时间/场景）实验，量化协议效应。模型还尝试了频率分解、跨任务监督、证据学习、对比损失和测试时自适应等变体但报告了负面结果。

Result: 帧级划分相比场景级划分使测试R_PSNR膨胀1.6-2.0 dB（p<0.01），延长训练后膨胀超过10 dB。非朗伯体不确定性通道与残差误差的相关系数r=0.67，是纹理通道的4倍以上。过滤掉75%最高不确定性像素可使保留像素的重构MSE降低77%，随机过滤无改善。模型达到15.98±0.41 dB R_PSNR，成本仅为5成员Deep Ensemble的五分之一，具备源分离不确定性的独特能力。

Conclusion: 倡导场景级划分作为MPI Sintel数据集的内在图像分解评估社区标准，并提供了六个代表性模型在该协议下的参考数值。提出的物理信息引导模型不仅能以较低成本达到接近Deep Ensemble的性能，还能提供源可分离的不确定性估计，且在真实照片上具有泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Evaluation protocols for learned intrinsic image decomposition on MPI Sintel have been inconsistent. Several prior works split the dataset by frames, which allows spatially similar frames of the same scene to appear in both train and test partitions. We quantify this leakage effect for the first time, across three architectures: a frame-level split inflates test R_PSNR by 1.6 to 2.0 dB (p less than 0.01 for all three, paired t-test across 3 seeds) relative to a scene-level split, confirming an architecture-independent protocol effect. A three-point gradient (random/temporal/scene) shows the gap is continuous, and under extended training the frame-level inflation exceeds 10 dB. We advocate scene-level splits as the community standard and provide reference numbers for six representative models under this protocol. As a case study within the corrected protocol, we present a physics-informed decomposition I = R composed with S + N with a source-separable three-way heteroscedastic uncertainty head. We empirically verify channel specialization: the non-Lambertian uncertainty channel shows r = 0.67 cross-correlation with non-Lambertian residual error, more than 4 times the texture channel's correlation. We further demonstrate downstream utility: filtering out the 75% highest-uncertainty pixels reduces reconstruction MSE by 77% on retained pixels, whereas random filtering produces no improvement. The specialization also holds on out-of-distribution real photographs. We report negative results for a more elaborate variant combining frequency decomposition, cross-task supervision, evidential learning, contrastive loss, and test-time adaptation. Our method reaches 15.98 plus or minus 0.41 dB R_PSNR, within 0.8 dB of a 5-member Deep Ensemble at one-fifth the cost, with the unique capability of source-separated uncertainty.

中文摘要翻译: 用于MPI Sintel上学习型内在图像分解的评估协议一直不一致。一些先前的工作按帧划分数据集，这使得同一场景中空间相似的帧同时出现在训练集和测试集中。我们首次量化了这种泄露效应，涵盖三种架构：相对于场景级划分，帧级划分使测试R_PSNR膨胀了1.6到2.0 dB（三种架构的p值均小于0.01，基于3个种子的配对t检验），确认了与架构无关的协议效应。一个三点梯度（随机/时间/场景）显示该差距是连续的，并且在延长训练后帧级膨胀超过10 dB。我们倡导场景级划分作为社区标准，并提供了六种代表性模型在该协议下的参考数值。作为纠正后协议内的一个案例研究，我们提出一个物理信息引导的分解 I = R∘S + N，带有一个源可分离的三路异方差不确定性头。我们经验性地验证了通道专业化：非朗伯体不确定性通道与非朗伯体残差误差的交叉相关系数r=0.67，是纹理通道相关性的4倍以上。我们进一步展示了下游实用性：过滤掉75%最高不确定性像素可使保留像素的重构MSE降低77%，而随机过滤没有产生任何改善。该专业化在分布外真实照片上也成立。我们报告了一个更复杂变体的负面结果，该变体结合了频率分解、跨任务监督、证据学习、对比损失和测试时自适应。我们的方法达到15.98±0.41 dB的R_PSNR，在五分之一成本内达到5成员Deep Ensemble的0.8 dB以内，并具有源分离不确定性的独特能力。

</details>


### [97] [Hybrid Pinching-Fluid Antenna Assisted Wireless Communications: Modeling and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06370)
*Xiao Lin,Yizhe Zhao,Xiangyang Wang,Halvin Yang,Bingxin Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合捏合-流体天线系统（HPFAS），在发射端使用捏合天线（PA）增强视距（LoS）信号，在接收端使用流体天线（FA）提供空间分集，联合利用两种天线优势，在各种传播条件下均优于单独使用PA或FA的系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可重构天线系统存在场景依赖性问题：流体天线在富散射环境中分集增益强但在视距主导条件下表现有限，而捏合天线可降低路径损耗但在非视距场景中性能差。需要一种能够联合利用LoS增强和空间分集的新型天线架构。

Method: 提出混合捏合-流体天线系统（HPFAS），发射端部署捏合天线（PA）以调整辐射点降低路径损耗，接收端部署流体天线（FA）以利用空间分集；建立了可处理的信道模型，推导了单用户和多用户场景下的中断概率表达式。

Result: 仿真结果验证了理论分析的准确性，表明所提出的HPFAS在各种传播条件下均一致优于仅使用捏合天线或仅使用流体天线的系统。

Conclusion: HPFAS通过联合利用PA的LoS增强能力和FA的空间分集能力，在不同传播条件下均表现出更优的性能，为可重构天线系统的设计提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Reconfigurable-antenna systems have received increasing attention for their ability to adapt wireless channels. However, existing architectures exhibit scenario-dependent limitations: fluid antennas provide strong diversity gains in rich-scattering environments but offer limited benefits under line-of-sight (LoS)-dominant conditions, while pinching antennas can effectively reduce path loss by adjusting the radiation point along a waveguide, yet perform poorly in severe non-LoS (NLoS) scenarios. This letter proposes a hybrid pinching-fluid antenna system (HPFAS), where pinching antenna (PA) is employed at the transmitter and a fluid antenna (FA) is used at the receiver to jointly exploit LoS enhancement and spatial diversity. A tractable channel model is developed, and outage probability expressions are derived for both single-user and multi-user scenarios. Simulation results validate the analysis and show that the proposed HPFAS consistently outperforms systems using only pinching antennas or only fluid antennas under various propagation conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构天线系统因其适应无线信道的能力而受到越来越多的关注。然而，现有架构存在场景依赖性限制：流体天线在富散射环境中可提供强分集增益，但在视距（LoS）主导条件下收益有限；而捏合天线虽能通过沿波导调整辐射点有效降低路径损耗，但在严重的非视距（NLoS）场景中性能表现较差。本文提出了一种混合捏合-流体天线系统（HPFAS），其中在发射端采用捏合天线（PA），在接收端采用流体天线（FA），以联合利用视距增强和空间分集。本文建立了一个可处理的信道模型，并推导了单用户和多用户场景下的中断概率表达式。仿真结果验证了理论分析的正确性，并表明所提出的HPFAS在各种传播条件下均一致优于仅使用捏合天线或仅使用流体天线的系统。

</details>


### [98] [Resource-Efficient CSI Prediction: A Gated Fusion and Factorized Projection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06578)
*Mohammad Hussain,Maedeh Adibag,Dilara Gurer,Gokhan Kalem,Kerim Serin,Sinem Coleri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合GRU编码器、Luong注意力、瓶颈门控融合模块和维度分离线性头（DSLH）的资源高效CSI预测器，在3GPP TR 38.901信道上以更少的参数和更高的推理吞吐量实现了平均NMSE为-13.84 dB的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动态MIMO系统中准确的信道状态信息（CSI）预测至关重要，但计算开销大，现有方法在精度与效率之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出资源高效的CSI预测器，包含：(1) GRU编码器结合Luong注意力提取时序特征与全局上下文；(2) 瓶颈门控融合模块集成局部循环特征与全局注意力信息；(3) 维度分离线性头（DSLH）降低输出映射的计算成本。

Result: 在3GPP TR 38.901标准信道上，平均NMSE为-13.84 dB，参数量减少26%，推理吞吐量提升约2.3倍，优于维度匹配的LinFormer基线。

Conclusion: 所提模型最适合LOS和混合条件场景，在中等序列长度的短时CSI预测中提供了实用的精度-效率权衡。

Original Abstract: Accurate Channel State Information (CSI) prediction is essential for dynamic multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems but remains computationally demanding. This letter proposes a resource-efficient predictor that combines a gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder with Luong attention, a bottleneck gated fusion module, and a Dimension-wise Separable Linear Head (DSLH). The gated fusion module integrates local recurrent features with global attention context, while the DSLH reduces the cost of the output mapping. Evaluated on 3GPP TR 38.901-compliant channels, the proposed model achieves an average NMSE of -13.84 dB with 26% fewer parameters and approximately 2.3x higher inference throughput than a dimension-matched LinFormer baseline. The proposed model is best suited to LOS and mixed-condition scenarios, offering a practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off for short-horizon CSI prediction at moderate sequence lengths.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的信道状态信息（CSI）预测对于动态多输入多输出（MIMO）系统至关重要，但计算需求仍然很高。本文提出了一种资源高效的预测器，该预测器结合了门控循环单元（GRU）编码器与Luong注意力机制、一个瓶颈门控融合模块以及一个维度分离线性头（DSLH）。门控融合模块将局部循环特征与全局注意力上下文集成在一起，而DSLH则降低了输出映射的计算成本。在符合3GPP TR 38.901标准的信道上进行评估，所提出的模型实现了平均NMSE为-13.84 dB，参数量比维度匹配的LinFormer基线减少26%，推理吞吐量提高约2.3倍。该模型最适合LOS和混合条件场景，在中等序列长度的短时CSI预测中提供了实用的精度-效率权衡。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）诊断，实现了0.8461的AUC，证明常规EHR数据可用于人群代表性CRS风险分层。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见且异质性强的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，其症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且异质表型进一步模糊了风险模式。既往预测研究多依赖单中心队列，缺乏人群层面的泛化能力。

Method: 利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向EHR数据，采用两年的诊断前历史预测CRS诊断。实施混合特征选择管道，结合基于患病率的统计筛选和基于模型的重要性排序，将约11万个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征。针对人口统计学异质性，在六个成人生性别和生命阶段亚组上训练分层模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达到0.8461，较最佳基线模型的判别能力提升了0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可以支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，并有助于在初级保健中实现更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Original Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

中文摘要翻译: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见的异质性炎症性疾病，导致显著的发病率和医疗成本。由于症状表现与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且异质表型进一步模糊了风险模式，CRS很难在常规诊疗中被早期识别。先前的预测研究通常依赖单中心队列，降低了人群层面的泛化能力。为克服这一问题，我们利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向电子健康记录（EHR）数据，使用两年的诊断前病史预测CRS诊断。为应对编码EHR数据中极端的特征稀疏性和高维问题，我们实施了一种混合特征选择管道，结合了基于患病率的统计筛选与基于模型的重要性排序，将约11万个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征。为捕获人口统计学异质性，我们在六个成人性别与生命阶段亚组上训练了人口统计学分层模型，并进行了亚组特异性超参数调优。我们的框架实现了0.8461的整体AUC，较最佳基线模型的判别能力提升了0.0168。这些结果表明，常规收集的EHR数据可以支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，并在初级保健中指导更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

</details>


### [100] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），一种无协调器的去中心化训练范式，通过分块坐标更新和序列感知优势估计器，使多个小模型组成的团队在性能上超越单个大模型，并支持即插即用地升级模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型参数多、部署成本高，现有工作尝试用多个小模型组成团队替代单个大模型，但联合更新多个智能体会产生复合分布偏移，导致训练中的协调与稳定性困难。

Method: 提出SAT框架，将团队表示为因子化策略，采用分块坐标更新方式按顺序更新智能体；开发序列感知的基于策略的优势估计器，结合每个智能体的KL信任域以隔离占用漂移；理论证明单调改进和即插即用不变性。

Result: 三个4B智能体（总计12B参数）组成的团队在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；将两个智能体替换为8B后，综合得分提升10.4%，验证了即插即用理论。

Conclusion: SAT框架实现了无中心控制器的可扩展去中心化训练，提供了单调改进和即插即用不变性的理论保证，实验表明小模型团队可超越大模型且支持灵活升级。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

中文摘要翻译: 拥有大量参数的大语言模型（LLM）虽然性能强劲，但部署成本往往高得令人望而却步。近期研究探索使用由更小、更高效的LLM组成的团队，这些模型集体能够匹配甚至超越单个大模型。然而，联合更新多个智能体会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练过程中的协调与稳定性困难。为此，我们提出顺序智能体微调（Sequential Agent Tuning, SAT），一种无需协调器的训练范式。SAT将团队表示为因子化策略，并采用对智能体进行分块坐标更新的方式，实现了无需中央控制器的可扩展去中心化训练。具体而言，我们开发了一种序列感知的、基于策略的优势估计器，该估计器以演化的团队策略为条件，并结合每个智能体的KL信任域以隔离占用漂移。理论上，该框架提供了两个关键保证：首先，它确保了单调改进，稳定了训练过程；其次，它建立了可证明的即插即用不变性：任何智能体都可以升级为更强的模型而无需重新训练团队其他成员，并且性能边界会得到改进的正式保证。实验上，由三个4B智能体（总计12B参数）组成的团队经过SAT训练后，在AIME24/25基准上平均超越更大的Qwen3-32B达3.9%。我们通过替换两个8B智能体验证了即插即用理论，综合得分提升了10.4%。我们在https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS提供代码和证明附录。

</details>


### [101] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了混沌动力学与预测多重性之间的理论联系，提出地平线约束Rashomon集框架，证明有效Rashomon集随预测时域指数收缩，并开发基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预测多重性和混沌动力学是机器学习中两个独立发展的基础挑战，尽管它们存在概念上的关联。现有研究缺乏对混沌系统中模型多重性如何随预测时域演化的理论理解，导致在安全关键的混沌领域中缺乏部署机器学习模型的理论指导。

Method: 1) 提出地平线约束Rashomon集理论框架，刻画混沌系统中模型多重性随预测时域的演化规律；2) 证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率随提前时间指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权度量，提供预测分歧的更紧界；4) 开发决策对齐的选择算法，根据下游效用而非单纯预测精度从近最优模型中选取模型。

Result: 在合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）和真实应用（风能、交通、天气）上的实验表明，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域中部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

Original Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

中文摘要翻译: 预测多重性和混沌动力学是机器学习中两个基础性挑战，尽管它们在概念上存在关联，但一直独立发展。我们通过引入地平线约束Rashomon集这一理论框架来弥合这一差距，该框架刻画了混沌系统中模型多重性随预测时域的演化规律。与静态预测任务中Rashomon集保持固定不同，混沌会引发初始相似模型之间的指数发散，从根本上改变了预测等价性的本质。我们证明了有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率随提前时间指数收缩，并引入了Lyapunov加权度量，为预测分歧提供了更紧的界。基于这些洞见，我们开发了决策对齐的选择算法，该算法根据下游效用而非仅依据预测精度从近最优模型中选取模型。在合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）和真实应用（风能、交通、天气）上的大量实验表明，我们的框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升了18-34%。本工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

</details>


### [102] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了概念引导（concept steering）的理论框架，证明了引导与仿射概念擦除之间的理论联系，提出了LEACE-Switch和MidSteer方法，并在多种模型（视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 概念引导方法在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全控制中取得了经验性成功，但缺乏全面的理论基础，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先建立引导与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；然后提出LEACE-Switch框架用于概念切换；在此基础上引入MidSteer，一种更通用的仿射框架，通过放松假设实现有向、最小扰动的概念操控。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上均表现良好，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过理论形式化和方法创新，本文为概念引导提供了坚实的理论基础，MidSteer作为一种通用且有效的仿射概念操控框架，在实践中具有广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

中文摘要翻译: 引导中间表示已成为控制生成模型的一种强大策略，尤其是在部署后的对齐和安全设置中。然而，尽管其经验成功，目前仍缺乏全面的理论框架。本文通过形式化概念引导的理论来填补这一空白。首先，我们建立了引导与仿射概念擦除之间的联系，证明了去除不良行为的标准方法是LEACE（一种闭式仿射擦除方法）的特例。接下来，我们为概念切换制定了一个原则性的理论框架——LEACE-Switch，并刻画了其提供最优仿射解所需满足的假设条件。在此基础上，我们引入了MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），一种更通用的仿射概念操控框架，它放松了这些假设，并支持有向、最小扰动的变换。我们证明了MidSteer在包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型在内的多种任务、模态和架构上均表现良好。

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种轻量级可学习门控机制，根据token难度动态跳过残差更新，在字符级语言建模上节省14-23%计算量且质量损失小于0.5%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token使用相同数量的层，忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 在相邻Transformer块之间的残差更新上添加一个轻量级两层MLP作为可学习的逐token门控，产生连续的停止概率，使机制端到端可微，仅增加1.7%参数且不改变基础架构。

Result: 在λ=0（无深度正则化）时，任务损失梯度单独驱动路由器跳过20%的token-层操作；在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上节省14-23% TLOps且质量损失<0.5%；在相同效率下验证损失比early exit低0.7%；学习到的路由可直接迁移到推理时稀疏执行，实现实际加速。

Conclusion: TSA能够学习与难度成比例的路由策略，在不改变基础架构且参数开销极小的前提下，有效减少计算量并保持模型质量，具备实际加速效益。

Original Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

中文摘要翻译: 标准的Transformer架构对每个token应用相同数量的层，而忽略了上下文难度差异。我们提出了Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种在相邻Transformer块之间的残差更新上的可学习逐token门控机制。每个门控是一个轻量级的两层多层感知机（MLP），产生连续的停止概率，使得该机制端到端可微，仅带来1.7%的参数开销且不改变基础架构。值得注意的是，TSA能够在没有任何显式深度惩罚的情况下学习与难度成比例的路由：即使在λ=0（无深度正则化）时，任务损失梯度单独驱动路由器跳过20%的token-层操作。在字符级语言建模中，TSA在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上节省了14-23%的token-层操作（TLOps），且质量损失低于0.5%。在相同效率下，TSA的验证损失比early exit方法低0.7%，并且学习到的路由可直接迁移到推理时的稀疏执行，实现实际的加速效果。

</details>


### [104] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架来分析稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征组合的不稳定性，揭示了在高维稀疏锥流形下存在组合坍缩阈值，并发现ReLU整流效应在组合过程中会产生类似棘轮效应的系统性漂移，从而限制了基于特征并集的激活操控的可扩展性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为解耦transformer架构中特征叠加的强大范式，但关于组合操控（即同时激活不同语义隐变量）的理论基础仍未被充分探索。现有的线性表示假设往往忽略了过完备字典中出现的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导了渐近组合坍缩阈值，该阈值由信号锥的高斯平均宽度（统计维度）表征；分析了高偏置下ReLU整流如何将微观相关性引起的方差波动转化为系统性的累积漂移，产生类似于棘轮效应的干扰增长。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集的结构化语义特征上验证了预测的缩放趋势，发现层次化相关性相对于随机基线加速了组合坍缩的转变。

Conclusion: 这些结果突显了基于特征并集（union）的激活操控在可扩展性上的几何约束，并推动了需要显式管理干扰、超越朴素线性叠加的组合机制的研究。

Original Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为一种强大的范式，用于解耦基于Transformer的架构中的特征叠加，从而通过激活操控实现精确控制。然而，组合操控（即同时激活不同的语义隐变量）的理论基础仍未被充分探索。主流的线性表示假设往往忽略了过完备字典中出现的非线性干扰效应。我们提出了一个几何框架来分析特征并集的不稳定性。将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，我们在球面字典模型下推导了一个渐近的组合-坍缩阈值，该阈值由信号锥的高斯平均宽度（统计维度）表征。我们进一步证明，在高偏置区域，ReLU整流将微观相关性引起的方差波动转化为在组合过程中累积的系统性漂移，产生类似于棘轮效应的干扰增长。我们在从CLEVR提取的结构化语义特征上验证了预测的缩放趋势，其中层次化相关性相对于随机基线加速了转变。总之，我们的结果突显了基于并集的操控在可扩展性上的几何约束，并推动了需要显式管理干扰、超越朴素线性叠加的组合机制的研究。

</details>


### [105] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现预训练权重会削弱现有UE方法的效果（语义过滤效应），并提出了一种层次化欺骗策略（浅层语义伪装，SSC），通过在语义有效子空间中生成扰动，在多种训练范式下保持数据不可学习性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要评估从头训练场景，忽略了广泛采用的预训练-微调范式，导致其在实际应用中的有效性未知。

Method: 提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）策略，将扰动生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中，通过层次化欺骗绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: 实验表明SSC在多种挑战性训练范式下（如浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练）均能一致保持数据的不可学习性，弥合了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键差距。

Conclusion: PF范式下冻结的浅层会保留数据语义，过滤不可学习噪声（语义过滤效应）；SSC通过语义子空间约束有效对抗该效应。

Original Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

中文摘要翻译: 在模型训练中未经授权使用个人数据已成为日益严重的隐私威胁。不可学习示例通过在良性示例中嵌入不易察觉的扰动来阻碍特征学习，从而解决这一问题。然而，现有研究主要在从头训练设置下评估不可学习示例，其在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的行为尚未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们首次对不同训练范式下的不可学习示例进行了系统研究。我们的分析表明，加载并冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有不可学习示例方法的有效性。我们进一步通过语义过滤来解释这些发现：虽然不可学习示例倾向于诱导模型过拟合非语义噪声，从而削弱其语义提取能力，但在预训练-微调范式下，冻结的浅层保留了数据语义，有效过滤了诸如不可学习噪声之类的干扰信息。受这些见解的启发，我们提出了一种层次化欺骗策略——浅层语义伪装（SSC），将生成过程限制在语义有效的子空间中，旨在绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。大量实验表明，我们的方法即使在具有挑战性的训练范式下（例如浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练）也能一致地保持数据的不可学习性，弥合了基于预训练的不可学习学习中的关键差距。

</details>


### [106] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS 提出了一种无训练的推理框架，通过熵加权负载和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决 MoE 多模态大模型在专家并行推理中因信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MoE MLLMs 在专家并行推理中因拖后腿效应存在严重效率瓶颈，而多模态场景下现存的基于 token 计数的负载均衡方法无法处理两类独特挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余视觉 token 与语义关键 token 被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配。

Method: (1) 熵加权负载机制：通过计算视觉 token 的熵来量化其语义价值，解决信息异构性问题；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制：根据输入实时的模态组成动态分配专家资源，解决模态动态性问题。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上，MACS 显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MACS 为 MoE MLLMs 在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了一种新颖且鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家多模态大语言模型（MoE MLLMs）在专家并行（EP）推理中因拖后腿效应面临严重的效率瓶颈。这一问题在多模态环境下进一步加剧，因为现存的基于 token 计数的负载均衡方法无法处理两类独特挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余的视觉 token 被与语义关键的 token 同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例的变化导致资源错配。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 MACS（模态感知容量缩放），一种无训练的推理框架。具体而言，MACS 引入熵加权负载机制来量化视觉 token 的语义价值，解决信息异构性问题；此外，动态模态自适应容量机制根据输入实时的模态组成分配专家资源。大量实验表明，MACS 在多种多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法，为 MoE MLLMs 在 EP 推理中的高效部署提供了一种新颖且鲁棒的解决方案。

</details>


### [107] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种监督内化方法，使推理模型能够在仅有结果监督的条件下，通过识别、修正和复用失败的推理轨迹来自动提取过程级学习信号，实现更细粒度的策略优化，从而将结果监督内化为过程监督。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖结果级奖励进行序列级优化导致信用分配困难，要么依赖外部构建的过程监督导致成本高昂且难以持续扩展。核心动机是解决如何在仅提供序列级结果监督的情况下，为推理的中间步骤生成细粒度学习信号。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，使模型在强化学习过程中自动识别、修正和复用失败的推理轨迹，从中提取过程级学习信号；并进一步抽象为一种新的训练范式，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并优化自身内部的过程监督。

Result: 论文主要在方法论层面提出新框架，未在摘要中提供具体实验结果数据，但强调该方法开辟了一条区别于外部提供过程监督的细粒度信用分配新路径。

Conclusion: 将推理强化学习理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题，通过监督内化方法实现仅在结果监督下的细粒度策略优化，为细化推理强化学习中的信用分配提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 推理强化学习的核心挑战不仅在于结果级监督的稀疏性，更根本的问题在于如何将仅在序列末尾提供的反馈转化为能够指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。现有方法要么依赖结果级奖励进行序列级优化，导致精准的信用分配困难；要么依赖外部构建的过程监督，成本高昂且难以可持续扩展。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新视角：推理强化学习可以理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题。基于这一视角，我们引入了一种面向推理强化学习的监督内化方法，使模型能够通过识别、修正和复用失败的推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，从而在仅有结果监督的条件下实现更细粒度的策略优化。我们进一步将这一思想抽象为一种新的训练范式，在该范式中，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并优化其自身的内部过程监督，开辟了一条与外部提供过程监督不同的、用于推理强化学习细粒度信用分配的新路径。

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT，一种在线自适应数据重加权框架，通过基于相似性质量信号的动态每样本学习率来替代传统的离线数据筛选与混合方法，在不改变训练样本数量的情况下实现隐式课程学习，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均优于离线方法和先前在线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据筛选与混合方法采用离线范式，与训练过程脱节，在模型/任务迁移时需重新运行整个流程，工程开销大；且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，容易损失数据多样性并损害泛化能力。

Method: 将数据筛选重新定义为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架，在训练过程中通过损失加权动态调整样本重要性，具体利用基于相似性的质量信号为每个样本分配自适应学习率，实现隐式课程学习，使模型逐步从粗粒度模式转向细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验上，ADAPT在相同FLOPs下一致优于离线数据筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权是一种比离线静态预处理更优的数据策展范式，ADAPT通过自适应每样本学习率实现了有效的隐式课程学习，为LLM训练中的数据策展提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

中文摘要翻译: 数据策展是大语言模型（LLM）训练中一个关键但探索不足的领域。现有方法（如数据筛选和混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程相脱节。这种分离带来了工程开销，并使策展变得脆弱：在模型或任务迁移时，整个流程必须重新运行。此外，离线方法通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，往往会牺牲数据多样性并损害泛化能力。我们提出将数据策展重新构想为一个在线重加权问题，即样本重要性在训练过程中通过损失加权动态调整，而非静态预处理。具体而言，我们引入了ADAPT（自适应数据重加权用于预训练和微调），这是一个动态在线框架，通过基于相似性质量信号的自适应每样本学习率对训练样本进行重加权，而不改变训练样本的数量。与强制执行静态数据分布的离线方法不同，ADAPT充当隐式课程学习器，随着模型的演化逐步将关注点从粗粒度模式转移到细粒度语义区分。在指令微调和大规模预训练上的实验表明，在相同FLOPs下，ADAPT一致优于离线筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

</details>


### [109] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于属性归因的持续微调框架，通过估计每个Transformer层中参数的逐元素重要性来指导梯度更新，从而缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有缓解灾难性遗忘的方法（如数据重放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知，无法区分哪些参数应该保留或更新。

Method: 提出属性引导的持续微调框架，估计每个Transformer层中任务特定、逐元素的参数重要性分数，并用这些分数调制梯度：对先前任务重要的参数进行较小更新，不相关的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试上，该方法一致优于基线方法，在保持旧任务性能的同时，在新任务上也取得了有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的属性引导持续微调框架能有效缓解LLM的灾难性遗忘问题，平衡了旧任务的保持与新任务的学习。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）在持续学习中常常遭受灾难性遗忘：在顺序学习新任务后，它们在早期任务上的表现会变差。现有方法通过数据重放、参数冻结或正则化来缓解灾难性遗忘。然而，这些方法缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知，因此无法区分应该保留或更新的参数。我们提出了一种属性引导的持续微调框架。我们的方法估计每个Transformer层中任务特定、逐元素的参数重要性，并使用这些分数来调制梯度。对先前任务重要的参数获得较小的更新，而不太相关的参数则保持可塑性以学习新任务。在持续学习基准测试上的实验表明，我们的方法一致优于基线方法，在更好地保留旧任务的同时，在新任务上保持了有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [110] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，平均仅需40次测试就能找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法中位数提升7%-32%，且评分速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 药物发现中，候选配体通过早期阶段的成功率不足5%，且需要针对缺乏先验数据的新型蛋白质进行高效配体筛选，因此亟需一种样本高效的方法。

Method: SPADE提出了一种新颖的配体选择策略，通过从稀疏数据中学习，以尽可能少的测试次数迭代选择候选配体，从而找到足够多的高质量配体。

Result: 平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在逐对比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为药物发现中针对新型蛋白质的配体筛选提供了一种高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

中文摘要翻译: 药物发现旨在寻找能与靶蛋白强结合且具有选择性的分子（配体）。然而，只有不到5%的候选配体能够通过药物发现的早期阶段。此外，我们需要适用于缺乏先验数据的新型蛋白质的方法。从头开始，我们必须迭代地选择和测试候选配体，以便在尽可能少的测试中找到足够数量的所需质量的配体。我们提出的算法名为SPADE，引入了一种新颖的配体选择方法，平均仅需40次测试就能找到10个高质量配体。在逐对比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%。此外，SPADE在评分候选药物时的速度比其最接近的竞争对手快10倍。数据集和代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md 获取。

</details>


### [111] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性逆问题（如修复、超分辨率）的零样本条件采样，分析了投影式扩散采样器因忽略切向得分而导致的偏差问题，提出投影-朗之万初始化与引导反向去噪相结合的方法，并通过信息论界保证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于投影的扩散采样器仅通过修正观测分量来保证测量一致性，但未能正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件采样结果存在偏差。作者希望从理论上分析这一偏差来源，并提出无偏的采样方法。

Method: 对得分函数进行法向-切向分解：观测量方向的得分由测量精确确定，仅切向条件得分未知。作者证明用无条件切向得分替代条件切向得分的误差上界由观测与未观测分量间的条件互信息决定。基于该理论，提出投影-朗之万初始化（projected-Langevin initialization）结合引导反向去噪（guided reverse denoising）的方法。

Result: 在理论上给出了切向得分替代误差的信息论上界，将其分解为初始化误差和路径得分失配误差；在修复和超分辨率实验上，所提方法优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过得分函数的法向-切向分解揭示了投影式扩散采样器偏差的信息论本质，提出的投影-朗之万初始化与引导反向去噪方法能有效提升线性逆问题中零样本条件采样的质量。

Original Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究使用预训练扩散模型对线性逆问题（包括图像修复和超分辨率）进行零样本条件采样。在这类问题中，观测仅决定未知信号的一部分，剩余的自由度需要根据正确的条件数据分布进行采样。现有的基于投影的采样器通过修正反向扩散过程中的观测分量来强制测量一致性。然而，仅凭测量一致性并不足以确定概率质量应如何沿可行集分布，这可能导致有偏的条件样本。我们通过得分函数的法向-切向分解来分析该问题。对于高斯加噪过程，观测方向上的得分由测量精确确定，只有切向条件得分是未知的。我们证明，用无条件切向得分替代该得分的误差上界由观测与未观测分量之间的无维数条件互信息决定。这给出了一个信息论上的分解，将误差分解为初始化误差和路径得分失配误差。受该理论启发，我们提出投影-朗之万初始化结合引导反向去噪的方法，在图像修复和超分辨率实验中，该方法优于强投影基线方法。

</details>


### [112] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将车辆路径问题中的路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，通过预编码边聚合、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，显著提升了多图场景下的可扩展性，在六个VRP变体上匹配或超越现有最优方法，且训练和推理速度更快。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，难以处理具有平行边的多图（如距离与时间的权衡），且已有方法存在严重可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构处理边选择阶段，并采用分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解的质量上匹配或超越现有最优方法，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF方法通过策略分解有效解决了多图VRP的可扩展性问题，在保持高质量解的同时大幅提升了效率。

Original Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数用于车辆路径问题（VRP）的神经方法局限于欧几里得设置或简单图。在这项工作中，我们转而考虑多图，其中平行边代表具有不同权衡（例如距离与时间）的 distinct 出行选项。很少有方法为这类形式化设计，而现有的方法面临严重的可扩展性问题。我们通过一种节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法缓解了这些可扩展性问题，该方法将路由策略分解为一个节点排列阶段和一个边选择阶段。为实现这种分解，我们引入了预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段，以及一种分层强化学习方法用于联合训练这两个阶段。我们在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解的质量上匹配或超越了现有最优方法，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

</details>


### [113] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含事件触发的半显式微分代数方程（DAE）的可微参数优化问题，提出了两种互补的梯度计算策略：自动微分-仿真方法（通过隐函数定理求解代数变量，并采用分段可微积分处理事件）和显式离散伴随方法（将前向仿真表示为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解拉格朗日乘子计算梯度）。两种方法在固定事件顺序和横向穿越条件下有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 含状态事件的DAE系统广泛存在于受代数方程约束且存在模式切换、冲击或状态重初始化的连续动力学系统中。此类系统的梯度学习极具挑战性，因为代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖于参数、重置映射带来不连续性，而现有方法对此处理不足。

Method: 本文提出了两种梯度计算方法：1）自动微分-仿真方法：在向量场内部求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理对代数求解过程求导，通过分段可微积分处理事件；2）显式离散伴随方法：将前向仿真建模为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子来计算梯度，阐明伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Result: 两种方法在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面进行了系统比较。两种方法都能为前向仿真所选择的事件路径提供梯度，且在固定事件顺序和横向穿越条件下有效。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架为DAE系统的事件驱动参数学习提供了理论基础和实用算法，明确了两种方法的适用场景和局限性，推动了可微仿真与事件驱动系统优化的结合。

Original Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

中文摘要翻译: 含状态事件的微分代数方程（DAEs）出现在系统的连续动力学受代数方程约束且被模式切换、开关逻辑、冲击或状态重初始化打断的情形中。此类系统的基于梯度的参数学习极具挑战性，因为代数变量是隐式定义的，事件时间依赖于参数，重置映射引入了不连续性。本文研究了含事件的半显式DAE的可微参数优化问题。我们将学习问题形式化为一个带DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题。然后我们开发了两种互补的梯度计算策略。第一种是自动微分-仿真方法，该方法在向量场内部求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理对代数求解过程求导，并通过分段可微积分处理事件。第二种是显式离散伴随方法，该方法将前向仿真表示为事件分割的残差系统，并通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子来计算梯度。该形式化阐明伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束，而非启发式惩罚项。我们从梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性四个维度比较了两种方法。两种方法都能为前向仿真所选择的事件路径提供梯度，且在固定事件顺序和横向守卫穿越条件下有效。

</details>


### [114] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WARDEN框架，通过分布鲁棒对抗训练动态重加权对抗样本，在保持模型效用的同时显著降低大语言模型的攻击成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大语言模型对抗训练方法计算成本高、难以扩展，而连续对抗训练方法虽有改进但仍有不足，需要一种更高效且能自动关注困难对抗样本的鲁棒对齐方法。

Method: 基于分布鲁棒优化，利用f-散度构建经验训练分布周围的模糊集，动态重加权对抗样本；通过凸对偶形式将目标函数简化为KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式，自动强调更难的对抗样本。

Result: 在多种大语言模型和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，且计算开销和模型效用与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用、可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，通过信息论目标函数在保持模型效用的同时有效提升对抗鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管在对齐和安全性方面取得了进展，大语言模型（LLM）仍然容易受到对抗性提示的影响，经常在新颖的攻击策略下表现出有害行为。虽然对抗训练可以提高鲁棒性，但现有方法计算成本高昂且难以扩展。最近的连续对抗训练方法，如连续对抗训练（CAT）和连续对抗偏好优化（CAPO），通过在嵌入空间中利用基于梯度的扰动来应对这一挑战，实现了更高效和更具表现力的攻击。基于这一范式，我们提出了WARDEN，一种面向大语言模型的分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，该框架通过经验训练分布周围的f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本。我们的方法在经验数据分布周围的散度球内优化最坏情况下的对抗损失，自动强调更难的对抗样本。利用凸对偶公式，该目标函数在KL散度下简化为log-sum-exp形式，并有一个动态参数控制重加权的强度。本研究引出了一类新的信息论目标函数，在保持模型效用的同时显著降低了攻击成功率。在多种大语言模型和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，其计算开销和模型效用成本与基于CAT、CAPO和MixAT的基线方法相当，使其成为一种实用的可扩展鲁棒对齐方法。

</details>


### [115] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了条件生成压缩感知问题，利用提示条件生成模型从子采样傅里叶测量中恢复图像，分离了提示在采样分布设计和恢复模型定义中的两种作用，理论证明提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持了近最优的生成压缩感知复杂度常数，提示不匹配则引入显式的兼容性惩罚，实验表明提示可作为具有不同影响的设计变量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成压缩感知利用预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型从有限测量中恢复结构化信号，但缺乏对条件版本（即利用提示条件生成模型进行图像恢复）的系统研究，尤其是提示在采样和恢复中的不同作用尚未被分离和分析。

Method: 1. 将提示条件生成压缩感知框架分解为两个角色：用于设计采样分布的提示和用于定义恢复模型的提示；2. 针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器，推导稳定恢复界；3. 引入Christoffel采样与提示匹配/不匹配的理论分析；4. 使用Stable Diffusion进行实验验证。

Result: 理论结果表明提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持了与现有近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数，而提示不匹配会引入显式的兼容性惩罚。Stable Diffusion实验显示提示能够有意义地重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复质量。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为设计变量，其在感知（采样分布设计）、逼近（恢复模型定义）和恢复三个环节中具有不同的影响效果。

Original Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

中文摘要翻译: 生成压缩感知利用预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型，从有限测量中恢复结构化信号。本文研究了该问题的条件版本，即使用提示条件生成模型从子采样傅里叶测量中进行图像恢复。我们的框架区分了条件的两种角色：用于设计采样分布的提示和用于定义恢复模型的提示。对于ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器，我们证明了稳定的恢复界，表明提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持了与现有近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数，而提示不匹配则会引入显式的兼容性惩罚。使用Stable Diffusion进行的实验表明，提示能够有意义地重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复。总体而言，我们的结果表明提示应被视为设计变量，其在感知、逼近和恢复环节中具有不同的影响。

</details>


### [116] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer模型在因果推理任务中进行微调时出现的灾难性模型坍塌问题，并提出了一种基于图逻辑约束和动态λ调度的语义损失函数来防止坍塌。实验表明，该方法在传递性和d-separation任务上显著优于完全坍塌的基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型于因果推理任务会导致模型坍塌——模型学会预测固定答案（如总是回答"Yes"或"No"），而并未真正学习到因果推理能力。现有方法未能有效解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 提出一种带有图结构逻辑约束和动态λ调度的语义损失函数（semantic loss function），通过引入基于图的逻辑约束来阻止模型学习琐碎的解，并利用动态λ调度平衡语义损失与标准训练损失。

Result: 在Gemma 270M模型上，标准微调在传递性和d-separation任务上100%坍塌，但准确率高达73.9%具有误导性。所提方法在传递性任务上达到70.4%准确率，d-separation任务上68.6%，相比坍塌基线提升42.7%。在1000个对抗性推理样本上，语义模型达到67-70%准确率，而坍塌模型仅为43-71%。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要而非可选的，提供了首个系统性解决因果推理微调中模型坍塌问题的方法。

Original Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

中文摘要翻译: 在因果推理任务上对Transformer模型进行标准微调会导致灾难性的模型坍塌，模型学会诸如无论输入结构如何总是预测"Yes"或"No"之类的琐碎解。我们证明，在不使用语义损失的情况下对Gemma 270M进行传递性和d-separation任务的微调，会导致100%的坍塌率，模型达到具有误导性的高准确率（73.9%），却未能学到任何因果推理能力。我们提出了一种带有图结构逻辑约束和动态λ调度的语义损失函数，可以防止这种坍塌。我们的方法在传递性任务上达到70.4%的准确率，在d-separation任务上达到68.6%的准确率，且预测稳定且依赖于上下文，相比坍塌基线提升了42.7%。在1000个结构推理样本上的对抗性评估显示，语义模型达到67-70%的准确率，而坍塌模型表现灾难性，仅为43-71%。我们通过在超过20万个评估样本上对五个模型变体进行综合基准测试验证了我们的发现，证明语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要而非可选的。

</details>


### [117] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，通过修改训练数据分布而非约束策略更新，来解决强化学习中策略评估与改进的“先有鸡还是先有蛋”问题。基于此方法设计了SV-API框架，并实例化为SV-PPO算法，在Atari和连续控制任务上匹配或超越PPO性能，同时执行更大的目标策略更新。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在强化学习中，安全改进策略要求价值函数在更新后的策略状态访问分布上保持准确，但该分布未知且无法采样。传统保守更新虽能解决问题，但会缩小策略更新幅度。本文旨在探索一种替代方案，通过修改训练分布而非约束策略更新来解决这一经典难题。

Method: 提出近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法：若训练数据分布近似于下一策略的分布，则条件满足。基于此，设计稳定值近似策略迭代（SV-API）框架，在标准近似策略迭代循环中固定目标策略，由迭代更新的行为策略收集相关经验，仅在满足收敛准则时才提交新策略。若满足特定稳定性准则，则保证更新安全；否则不劣于标准API。将SV-API应用于PPO得到SV-PPO。

Result: 在高维离散控制（Atari）和连续控制基准任务上，SV-PPO匹配或超越了PPO的性能，同时执行了显著更大的目标策略更新。实验结果证明了ANPS作为经典RL挑战新解决方案的可行性。

Conclusion: ANPS通过修改训练分布而非约束策略更新的方式，为解决RL中策略评估与改进的耦合问题提供了一种可行且有效的新方案。SV-API框架及其实例化SV-PPO算法的成功，表明了该方法的实际应用潜力。

Original Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

中文摘要翻译: 我们重新审视了强化学习中一个经典的“先有鸡还是先有蛋”问题：为了安全地改进策略，价值函数必须在更新后策略的状态访问分布上保持准确。然而，该状态分布是未知的，且无法为训练价值函数的目的而采样。保守更新解决了这一问题，但代价是缩小了策略更新幅度。本文探索了一种替代方案——近似下一策略采样（ANPS），该方法通过修改训练分布而非约束策略更新来解决问题。如果训练数据的分布近似于下一策略的分布，则ANPS条件得到满足。为了证明ANPS的可行性和有效性，我们提出了稳定值近似策略迭代（SV-API）。SV-API修改了标准的近似策略迭代循环，在迭代更新的行为策略收集相关经验期间保持目标策略固定，仅在满足收敛准则后才提交新策略。如果满足特定稳定性准则，则保证更新是安全的；否则，其安全性也不劣于标准近似策略迭代。将SV-API应用于PPO得到稳定值PPO（SV-PPO），该算法在高维离散控制（Atari）和连续控制基准任务上匹配或超越了PPO的性能，同时执行了显著更大的目标策略更新。这些结果证明了ANPS作为RL中这一经典挑战的新解决方案的可行性。

</details>


### [118] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习在智能体数量增多时计算与通信成本过高的问题，提出了一种闭式最优去中心化策略，基于最差客户端/极小极大准则最小化弱势智能体的遗憾，并证明在大规模极限下该策略渐近收敛于不可扩展计算的纳什最优中心化策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模联邦学习中，智能体数量激增导致计算和通信成本难以扩展，而现有中心化策略的计算不可扩展，亟需一种可扩展的去中心化智能策略。

Method: 推导出闭式最优去中心化策略，基于最差客户端/极小极大准则最小化弱势智能体的在线遗憾；采用在线加权机制优化服务器计算的客户端预测混合，同时优化均值预测和最弱客户端预测。

Result: 理论证明该去中心化策略在大规模极限下渐近收敛于纳什最优中心化策略；数值实验验证了理论保证，并表明该策略通常优于贪心去中心化基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式最优去中心化策略在可扩展性和最优性之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模联邦学习提供了一种高效且理论上保证的解决方案。

Original Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 在大规模AI的现代时代，联邦学习已成为训练大规模AI智能体群体日益重要的工具；然而，其计算和通信成本会随着智能体数量增加而迅速失效。这正是去中心化智能策略的用武之地：每个智能体自主行动，仅使用自身状态以及集成体（即均值场）的最小化摘要。我们以闭式形式推导出了唯一的最优去中心化策略。最优性通过最差客户端/极小极大准则来刻画：最小化弱势智能体的遗憾，即集成体中最弱智能体所承担的最大在线成本。我们进一步证明，在大规模极限下，所得到的去中心化策略渐近收敛于纳什最优中心化策略，而后者的直接计算是不可扩展的。我们使用在线加权机制来优化服务器计算的客户端预测混合，从而在之前优化的最弱客户端预测基础上进一步改善均值预测。数值实验验证了我们的理论保证，并表明我们的去中心化策略通常优于自然的贪心去中心化基线方法。

</details>


### [119] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线局部化共形预测（OLCP）及其带宽自适应变体OLCP-Hedge，在非交换数据（在线学习/时间序列）场景下实现带协变量依赖的局部校准，在保证长期覆盖率的同时生成更窄的预测集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线共形方法（如ACI）仅依赖全局校准，在协变量存在异质性时效率低下，无法根据协变量信息自适应调整预测区间宽度。

Method: 提出OLCP，将在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化相结合；进一步提出OLCP-Hedge，将带宽选择建模为在线专家聚合问题，通过约束在线凸优化框架实现带宽自适应选择。

Result: 两种算法均具有长期覆盖率保证；仿真和真实数据实验表明，相比现有基线方法，所提方法在保证有效长期覆盖率的同时生成了更窄的预测集。

Conclusion: 通过局部化校准有效解决了协变量异质性下的低效问题，带宽的自适应选择进一步降低了超参数敏感性，提升了方法的实用性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 共形预测是一个为可交换数据上的通用模型提供有效不确定性量化的框架。然而，在线学习和时间序列场景下，可交换性条件并不满足。现有的在线共形方法（如自适应共形推断ACI）虽能实现长期有效性，但由于依赖全局校准，在协变量异质性下仍然效率低下。本文提出在线局部化共形预测（OLCP），该方法将在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化相结合，以更好地反映异质性。为了降低对局部化带宽的敏感性，我们进一步提出OLCP-Hedge，通过约束在线凸优化框架将带宽选择建模为在线专家聚合问题。重要的是，我们为两种算法提供了覆盖率保证，并通过仿真和真实数据实验证明，所提方法在保证有效长期覆盖率的同时，相比现有基线方法生成了更窄的预测集。

</details>


### [120] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非短视路径策略梯度（NM-PPG）方法，通过连续松弛和直通式采样实现主动特征获取（AFA）中非短视采集策略的端到端优化，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在主动特征获取问题中，现有方法多为短视的贪心策略或基于得分函数策略梯度的高方差方法，缺乏能够端到端优化长期采集策略的低方差非短视方法。

Method: 将AFA建模为POMDP，提出NM-PPG方法：(1) 对离散特征采集过程进行连续松弛，使得完整采集轨迹可进行路径梯度计算；(2) 设计直通式采样方案，前向传播执行硬采集、反向传播通过软松弛回传梯度；(3) 采用熵正则化与分阶段温度锐化来稳定优化。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，NM-PPG的性能优于当前最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG通过路径梯度与直通式采样有效解决了非短视AFA策略的端到端优化问题，降低了方差并提升了性能。

Original Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy.
